# Amsterdam run 37 — dip bot run report

*dip25: pre-send 0.696 SOL + age-1 skip + QUIET3 (fol 4.0, no fbots, stop 5:2), regular paper, observatory, profit lock 0.65/30%*

> **Verdict:** +5.102 SOL on chain (8.879050 -> 13.981228) in 18 h 23 min (10-29 07:33 -> 10-30 01:56 PKT, stopped by the owner to start run 38 with live re-arm + Astralane A/B). 338 pre-send fills, realized +5.41, rent back ~+0.5, failed-try fees -0.30. Best run by far (~6.7 SOL/day). Peak +5.43 (22:38), lock floor reached +3.80. Biggest win LUQCuX +0.566, biggest loss D3TDuR -0.200. Filters (try-level replay): create_slot +3.46, QUIET +0.82, age-1 +0.38 saved over 17.7 h. Paper audit: dipmon paper line measures all triggers at 20 tries/slot (negative by design); shadow bug: own trades in its tape. Re-arm calibrated replay: +2.67 SOL at 0.40, +4.53 at 0.65 over 17.7 h. End check: 0 held (165 'unreadable' = closed accounts; on-chain 0 non-zero token accounts).

## 4b. Pre-send live (pre-placed buys) — separate from the reactive bot
Buys pre-sent after a big whale buy, before any dump. **Not included in sections 5–11**, which cover only the reactive dip bot. Realized = the engine's PRESEND LIVE REALIZED records (chain txs of each buy and sell; the buy pays the token-account rent). Rent back = that rent returned when the engine closed each pre-send account after the exit (dip19+; before dip19 the sell closed it and the refund sat inside realized). Failed tries = fees of tries that landed and failed (6042 price, deadline, …). Net = realized + rent back − failed tries.
